Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : *STLM-30
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK10A
Aciklama : ÖZEL AMBAR RAPORU V.3.1
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi



Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=TARIH	KRITERTIPI=?	SORUACIKLAMA=Baþlangýç Tarihi	DEFVALUE01=1 08	
FIELDNAME1=TARIH	KRITERTIPI=?	SORUACIKLAMA=Bitiþ Tarihi	DEFVALUE01=31 08	
FIELDNAME1=KOD	KRITERTIPI=><	
FIELDNAME1=AMBCODE	KRITERTIPI==	DEFVALUE01=HM	
FIELDNAME1=KOD_STOK00_GK_1	KRITERTIPI=S05	
FIELDNAME1=EVRAKTYPE	KRITERTIPI=S13	DEFVALUE01=SI	DEFVALUE02=RI	
FIELDNAME1=DV	KRITERTIPI=<>	DEFVALUE01=0	


Baglantili Tablolar Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=KOD	JOINEDTABLE=STOK00	JOINEDFIELD=KOD	


Ekran Formu Sekmesi



Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi


FIELDNAME1=DV	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=DIM SAY
IF RAPOR.TARIH < KRITERGIRIS.BTARIH OR RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE = "0S" OR RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE="SI" THEN
	RAPOR.DV = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
	SAY=SAY+1
	'MSGBOX RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR & " = " & SAY
END IF
IF RAPOR.TARIH >= KRITERGIRIS.BTARIH AND RAPOR.TARIH <= KRITERGIRIS.ETARIH THEN
	SELECT CASE TRIM(RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE)
		CASE "SI"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)<>"1" THEN
				RAPOR.G1 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.GIRIS1=1
			END IF
		CASE "RI"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)="1" THEN
				RAPOR.C1=RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.CIKIS1=1				
			END IF
		CASE "SF"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)<>"1" THEN
				RAPOR.G2 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.GIRIS2=1
			END IF
		CASE "IF"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)= "1" THEN
				RAPOR.C2=RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.CIKIS2=1				
			END IF
		CASE "I2"
			RAPOR.G3 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.GIRIS3=1
		CASE "TR"
			RAPOR.G4 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.GIRIS4=1
		CASE "FG"
			RAPOR.G5 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.GIRIS5=1
		CASE "UT"
			RAPOR.C3 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS3=1				
		CASE "F2"
			RAPOR.C4 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS4=1				
		CASE "YC"
			RAPOR.C5 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS5=1				
		CASE "II"
			RAPOR.C6 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS6=1				
		CASE "T2"
			RAPOR.C7 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS7=1				
		CASE "DG"
			RAPOR.C8 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
		CASE "DT"
			RAPOR.C9 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
		CASE "KD"
			RAPOR.C10 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
	END SELECT	
END IF	ACIKLAMA=Devir Mik

RAPOR.GIRISSAYISI=RAPOR.GIRIS1+RAPOR.GIRIS2+RAPOR.GIRIS3+RAPOR.GIRIS4+RAPOR.GIRIS5

'RAPOR.DVT=(RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR+RAPOR.DV)/2

	RAPOR.RTARIH=S10.TARIH

FIELDNAME1=ADET	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ADET = 1	
FIELDNAME1=ADET2	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ADET2 = 1	
FIELDNAME1=SIPTESLIMTARIHI	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.SIPTESLIMTARIHI = RAPOR.REV_RTESTARIH	
FIELDNAME1=TESLIMTARIHI	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TESLIMTARIHI = Doc.STOK46A_AVGTESLIMTARIHI(RAPOR.ARTNO)	ACIKLAMA=Satýnalma Sipariþi Satýrý Ortalama Teslim Tarihi	
FIELDNAME1=FARK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.FARK = (Doc.cDateToInt(RAPOR.SIPTESLIMTARIHI) - Doc.cDateToInt(RAPOR.TESLIMTARIHI))/RAPOR.ADET2	
FIELDNAME1=ART_TESLIM_PUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=if RAPOR.FARK>= 0 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 35
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK >= -3 and RAPOR.FARK < -4 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 35
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK = -4 Then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 15
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK = -5 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 15
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK = -6 Then 
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 10
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK = -7 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 10
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK = -8 Then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 7
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK = -9 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 7
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK = -10 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 7
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK <= -11 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 3
end if	
FIELDNAME1=MIKTARFARK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.MIKTARFARK = RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR - RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR	
FIELDNAME1=KABULM	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=IF RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_QCONAY="E" THEN
	RAPOR.KABULM=RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR
ELSEIF RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_QCONAY="S" THEN
	RAPOR.SARTLIM=RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR
ELSEIF RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_QCONAY="I" THEN
	RAPOR.REDM=RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=SARTLIM	
FIELDNAME1=REDM	
FIELDNAME1=FAZLA	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=if RAPOR.MIKTARFARK > 0 then
	RAPOR.FAZLA = 1
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTARFARK < 0 then
	RAPOR.AZ = 1
Else
	RAPOR.TAM = 1
End if	
FIELDNAME1=TAM	
FIELDNAME1=AZ	
FIELDNAME1=MIKTAR_ORAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR, RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR) * 100,0)	
FIELDNAME1=ART_MIKTAR_PUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=if RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 95 AND RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <=105 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 25
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 90 AND RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <=94 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 15
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 80 AND RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <=89 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 10
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 106 AND RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <=110 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 15
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 111 AND RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <=120 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 10
ELSE
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 0
End if	
FIELDNAME1=SISTEM_BELGESI	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.SISTEM_BELGESI = Doc.GetUDFValue("07",RAPOR.CH_KODU,"BELGE",0)	
FIELDNAME1=TAKDIR	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TAKDIR = Doc.GetUDFValue("07",RAPOR.CH_KODU,"TAKDIR",0)	
FIELDNAME1=KPUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT='RAPOR.KPUAN=DOC.DIV(RAPOR.KABULM,RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR)*0.25*100
'RAPOR.SPUAN=DOC.DIV(RAPOR.SARTLIM,RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR)*0.15*100
'RAPOR.RPUAN=RAPOR.REDM*0

RAPOR.KPUAN=DOC.DIV(RAPOR.KABULM,RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR)*0.25*100
RAPOR.SPUAN=DOC.DIV(RAPOR.SARTLIM,RAPOR.ARTNO_STOK29T_SF_MIKTAR)*0.15*100
RAPOR.RPUAN=RAPOR.REDM*0	
FIELDNAME1=SPUAN	
FIELDNAME1=RPUAN	
FIELDNAME1=KALITEPUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.KALITEPUAN=RAPOR.KPUAN+RAPOR.SPUAN+RAPOR.RPUAN+RAPOR.SISTEM_BELGESI	
FIELDNAME1=TEDPUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TEDPUAN=RAPOR.KALITEPUAN+RAPOR.SISTEM_BELGESI+RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN+RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN+RAPOR.TAKDIR	
FIELDNAME1=TSINIF	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=IF RAPOR.TEDPUAN >=75 Then
	RAPOR.TSINIF= "A"
ELSEIF RAPOR.TEDPUAN >=50 AND RAPOR.TEDPUAN <=74 Then
	RAPOR.TSINIF=" B"
ELSEIF RAPOR.TEDPUAN >=25 AND RAPOR.TEDPUAN <=49 Then
	RAPOR.TSINIF= "C"
ELSEIF RAPOR.TEDPUAN <=24 Then
	RAPOR.TSINIF= "D"
END IF

	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 25
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK >= -4 and RAPOR.FARK <= -5 then
	RAPOR.ART_TESLIM_PUAN = 10
ElseIf RAPOR.FARK >= -6 and RAPOR.FARK <= -7 then

FIELDNAME1=ART_MIKTAR_PUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=if RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 95 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 105 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 20
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 90 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 94 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 10
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 84 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 93 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 8
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 70 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 79 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 6
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 60 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 69 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 2
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 106 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 110 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 10
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 111 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 120 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 8
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 121 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 130 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 6
Elseif RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN >= 131 and RAPOR.MIKTAR_ORAN <= 140 Then
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 4
Else
	RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN = 0
End if	
FIELDNAME1=MIKTAR_PUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.MIKTAR_PUAN = Doc.Div(RAPOR.ART_MIKTAR_PUAN, RAPOR.ADET2)	
FIELDNAME1=BELGEP	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.BELGEP = Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",TRIM(RAPOR.CH_KODU),"BELGE",0)	
FIELDNAME1=TESLIM_ALINAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim S60T, FasonMiktar, SIMiktar, QStr2,Qstr,S29T,S68T
FasonMiktar = 0
SIMiktar = 0
QStr="CARIHESAPCODE='"+trim(RAPOR.CH_KODU)+"'&TARIH>='"+KRITERGIRIS.B_REV_RTESTARIH+"'&TARIH<='"+KRITERGIRIS.E_REV_RTESTARIH+"'"
QStr2="CHCODE='"+trim(RAPOR.KOD)+"'&TARIH>='"+KRITERGIRIS.B_REV_RTESTARIH+"'&TARIH<='"+KRITERGIRIS.E_REV_RTESTARIH+"'"
Set S29T = Doc.Select1("BB","STOK29T",QStr,"",-1)
if S29T.GetRecCount() = 0 Then
	SIMiktar = 0
Else
	S29T.GroupBy("CARIHESAPCODE")
	SIMiktar= S29T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
End if
Set S68T = Doc.Select1("BB","STOK68T",QStr2,"",-1)
if S68T.GetRecCount() = 0 Then
	FasonMiktar = 0
Else
	S68T.GroupBy("CHCODE")
	FasonMiktar= S68T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
End if

RAPOR.TESLIM_ALINAN = SIMiktar + FasonMiktar
QStr="IADEMI='1'&CH_KODU='"+trim(RAPOR.CH_KODU)+"'&TARIH>='"+KRITERGIRIS.B_REV_RTESTARIH+"'&TARIH<='"+KRITERGIRIS.E_REV_RTESTARIH+"'"
Set S60T = Doc.Select1("BB","STOK60T",QStr,"",-1)
if S60T.GetRecCount() = 0 Then
	RAPOR.IADE = 0
Else
	S60T.GroupBy("CH_KODU")
	RAPOR.IADE= S60T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
End if	
FIELDNAME1=IADE	
FIELDNAME1=PPM	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.PPM = Doc.Div(RAPOR.IADE,RAPOR.TESLIM_ALINAN) * 1000000	
FIELDNAME1=FIYAT	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.FIYAT = Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00",RAPOR.CH_KODU,"FIYAT",0)	
FIELDNAME1=PPM_PUANI	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=if RAPOR.TESLIM_ALINAN = 0 then
	RAPOR.PPM_PUANI = 0
Elseif RAPOR.PPM <= 100 Then
	RAPOR.PPM_PUANI = 25
Elseif RAPOR.PPM <= 500 then
	RAPOR.PPM_PUANI = 10
Elseif RAPOR.PPM <= 1000 then
	RAPOR.PPM_PUANI = 9
Elseif RAPOR.PPM <= 10000 then
	RAPOR.PPM_PUANI = 5
Else
	RAPOR.PPM_PUANI = 0
end if	
FIELDNAME1=TOPLAM_PUAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TOPLAM_PUAN = RAPOR.TESLIM_PUAN + RAPOR.MIKTAR_PUAN + RAPOR.BELGEP+RAPOR.PPM_PUANI+RAPOR.FIYAT	
FIELDNAME1=TOTAL_RAPORADET	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TOTAL_RAPORADET=RAPOR.TOTALA("ADET2",ROW,0)	
FIELDNAME1=ORT	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ORT=DOC.DIV(RAPOR.TOPLAM_PUAN,RAPOR.TOTAL_RAPORADET)

Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : *TEST-BS
Hangi Tablo icin : MUHDST
Aciklama : KDV LÝSTESÝ (CF VE NK)
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi

SELECT MUHDST.EVRAKTYPE, MUHDST.EVRAKNO, MUHDST.TRNUM, MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE, MUHDST.MUHASEBEFIYAT
FROM MUHDST
WHERE 1=1 
AND (1=0 
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL01_EVRAKTYPE###
	OR MUHDST.EVRAKTYPE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL01_EVRAKTYPE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL02_EVRAKTYPE###
	OR MUHDST.EVRAKTYPE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL02_EVRAKTYPE###
	###ENDIF###
	)
AND (1=0 
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL01_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL01_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL02_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL02_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL03_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL03_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL04_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL04_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL05_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL05_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	)

Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=EVRAKTYPE	KRITERTIPI=S02	DEFVALUE01=CF	DEFVALUE02=NK	
FIELDNAME1=MUHASEBECODE	KRITERTIPI=S05	SORUACIKLAMA=Sýrasýyla %1, %8, %18 Ýndirilecek KDV Hesaplarýný giriniz	DEFVALUE01=191 01 001	DEFVALUE02=191 01 008	
FIELDNAME1=TARIH	KRITERTIPI=><	


Baglantili Tablolar Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=MUHASEBECODE	JOINEDTABLE=MUHA00	JOINEDFIELD=KOD	RESULTFIELD=AD	


Ekran Formu Sekmesi



Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi


FIELDNAME1=CH_KODU	
FIELDNAME1=CH_ADI	
FIELDNAME1=VD	
FIELDNAME1=VNO	
FIELDNAME1=MATRAH	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim Tutar, Oran
if TRIM(RAPOR.MUHASEBECODE) = TRIM(KRITERGIRIS.CL01_MUHASEBECODE) Then
	Oran = 1
Elseif TRIM(RAPOR.MUHASEBECODE) = TRIM(KRITERGIRIS.CL02_MUHASEBECODE) Then
	Oran = 8
Elseif TRIM(RAPOR.MUHASEBECODE) = TRIM(KRITERGIRIS.CL03_MUHASEBECODE) Then
	Oran = 18
End if
IF RAPOR.VERGIMATRAHI=0 THEN
	RAPOR.MATRAH = Doc.Div(RAPOR.MUHASEBEFIYAT * 100, Oran)
ELSE
	RAPOR.MATRAH=RAPOR.VERGIMATRAHI
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=TARIH	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=if RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE = "CF" Then
	RAPOR.TARIH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI20E", "TARIH", RAPOR.EVRAKNO, "")
	RAPOR.ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI20T", "ACIKLAMA", RAPOR.EVRAKNO+RAPOR.OR_TRNUM, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	RAPOR.CH_KODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI20T", "KOD", RAPOR.EVRAKNO+RAPOR.OR_TRNUM, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
 	RAPOR.CH_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", RAPOR.CH_KODU, "")
 	RAPOR.VD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", RAPOR.CH_KODU, "")
 	RAPOR.VNO = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", RAPOR.CH_KODU, "")
Elseif RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE = "NK" Then
	RAPOR.TARIH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("NAKT11E", "TARIH", RAPOR.EVRAKNO, "")
	RAPOR.ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("NAKT10T", "ACIKLAMA", RAPOR.EVRAKNO+RAPOR.OR_TRNUM, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
End if	
FIELDNAME1=ACIKLAMA	
FIELDNAME1=KDV_DAHIL_TUTAR	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.KDV_DAHIL_TUTAR = RAPOR.MATRAH + RAPOR.MUHASEBEFIYAT

FIELDNAME1=ADET	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim S40T
Set S40T=Doc.Select1("dsfsd","STOK40T","TEKLIFARTNO='"&RAPOR.ARTNO&"'","",-1)
IF RAPOR.ACIKKAPALI="K" AND S40T.GETRECCOUNT=1 THEN
	RAPOR.ADET=1
	RAPOR.KAZANILANTUTAR=RAPOR.OR_TUTAR
ELSEIF RAPOR.ACIKKAPALI="K" AND S40T.GETRECCOUNT=0 THEN
	RAPOR.ADET1=1
	RAPOR.KAYBEDILENTUTAR=RAPOR.OR_TUTAR
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=ADET1	
FIELDNAME1=TOPLAM	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TOPLAM=RAPOR.ADET+RAPOR.ADET1	
FIELDNAME1=ORAN	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ORAN=DOC.DIV(RAPOR.ADET,RAPOR.TOPLAM)*100	
FIELDNAME1=KAZANILANTUTAR	
FIELDNAME1=KAYBEDILENTUTAR	
FIELDNAME1=TOPLAMTUTAR	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TUTAR_ORAN=DOC.DIV(RAPOR.KAZANILANTUTAR,RAPOR.OR_TUTAR)*100	
FIELDNAME1=TUTAR_ORAN

----------------------------------------------------------------
Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : MALIYET
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK10A
Aciklama : STOK MALIYETLI ENVANTER RAPORU DETAYLI
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi



Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=TARIH	KRITERTIPI=?	SORUACIKLAMA=Baþlangýç tarihi	
FIELDNAME1=TARIH	KRITERTIPI=<=	SORUACIKLAMA=Bitiþ tarihi	
FIELDNAME1=KOD	KRITERTIPI=><	
FIELDNAME1=AMBCODE	KRITERTIPI=S07	
FIELDNAME1=AMBCODE	KRITERTIPI=NS05	


Baglantili Tablolar Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=KOD	JOINEDTABLE=STOK00	JOINEDFIELD=KOD	


Ekran Formu Sekmesi



Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi


FIELDNAME1=MALIYET	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.MALIYET = RAPOR.TL_TUTAR + RAPOR.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR	
FIELDNAME1=OS	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=IF MID(KRITERGIRIS.BTARIH, 6, 5)="01/01" THEN
	IF RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE = "0S" THEN
			RAPOR.OS = RAPOR.OS + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.OS_TL = RAPOR.OS_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
	END IF
ELSE
	IF RAPOR.TARIH < KRITERGIRIS.BTARIH THEN
			RAPOR.OS = RAPOR.OS + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.OS_TL = RAPOR.OS_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
	END IF
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=OS_TL	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=IF RAPOR.TARIH >= KRITERGIRIS.BTARIH THEN
SELECT CASE RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE 
			CASE "SI"
				IF RAPOR.IADEMI <> "1" THEN
					RAPOR.SI = RAPOR.SI + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.SI_TL = RAPOR.SI_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				ELSE
					RAPOR.SIA = RAPOR.SIA + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.SIA_TL = RAPOR.SIA_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				END IF
			CASE "SF"
				IF RAPOR.IADEMI <> "1" THEN
					RAPOR.SI = RAPOR.SI + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.SI_TL = RAPOR.SI_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				ELSE
					RAPOR.SIA = RAPOR.SIA + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.SIA_TL = RAPOR.SIA_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				END IF
			CASE "RI"
				IF RAPOR.IADEMI <> "1" THEN
					RAPOR.RI = RAPOR.RI + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.RI_TL = RAPOR.RI_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				ELSE
					RAPOR.RIA = RAPOR.RIA + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.RIA_TL = RAPOR.RIA_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				END IF
			CASE "IF"
				IF RAPOR.IADEMI <> "1" THEN
					RAPOR.RI = RAPOR.RI + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.RI_TL = RAPOR.RI_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				ELSE
					RAPOR.RIA = RAPOR.RIA + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.RIA_TL = RAPOR.RIA_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				END IF

			CASE  "DG"
				IF RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR > 0 THEN
					RAPOR.DT = RAPOR.DT + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.DT_TL = RAPOR.DT_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				ELSE
					RAPOR.DG = RAPOR.DG + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.DG_TL = RAPOR.DG_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				END IF
			CASE  "DT"
				IF RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR > 0 THEN
					RAPOR.DT = RAPOR.DT + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.DT_TL = RAPOR.DT_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				ELSE
					RAPOR.DG = RAPOR.DG + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.DG_TL = RAPOR.DG_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
				END IF
			CASE "MG"
					RAPOR.MG = RAPOR.MG + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.MG_TL = RAPOR.MG_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
			CASE "M2"
					RAPOR.M2 = RAPOR.M2 + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.M2_TL = RAPOR.M2_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET	
FIELDNAME1=SI	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=CASE "FG"
					RAPOR.FG = RAPOR.FG + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.FG_TL = RAPOR.FG_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
			CASE "F2"
					RAPOR.F2 = RAPOR.F2 + RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
					RAPOR.F2_TL = RAPOR.F2_TL + RAPOR.MALIYET
END SELECT
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=SI_TL	
FIELDNAME1=SIA	
FIELDNAME1=SIA_TL	
FIELDNAME1=RI	
FIELDNAME1=RI_TL	
FIELDNAME1=RIA	
FIELDNAME1=RIA_TL	
FIELDNAME1=FG	
FIELDNAME1=FG_TL	
FIELDNAME1=F2	
FIELDNAME1=F2_TL	
FIELDNAME1=DG	
FIELDNAME1=DG_TL	
FIELDNAME1=DT	
FIELDNAME1=DT_TL	
FIELDNAME1=MG	
FIELDNAME1=MG_TL	
FIELDNAME1=M2	
FIELDNAME1=M2_TL

----------------------------------------------------------------
Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : PROJE-01
Hangi Tablo icin : BOMU01T
Aciklama : ÜRÜN AÐACINI FEATURE OPTIONS A GÖRE LÝSTE
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi

SELECT BOMTRV.*, STOK00.AD KAYNAKKODADI, STOK00.IUNIT KAYNAKKODBIRIMI FROM BOM_TRAVERSE00(10, '40 06 01 0075', '1', '', '001848') BOMTRV 

	LEFT JOIN BOMU01E ON BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = BOMTRV.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE AND BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = '*' 
	LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD = BOMTRV.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE  
	
	WHERE 1=1  

	--AND SEVIYE = 1 

	--AND LEFT(BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE,2) <> '10' 
	--AND BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = 'H' 
	AND BOMU01E.EVRAKNO <> ''

Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi



Baglantili Tablolar Sekmesi



Ekran Formu Sekmesi



Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi


FIELDNAME1=SEVIYE	
FIELDNAME1=KAYNAKKODADI

Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : STOK-01
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK00
Aciklama : YGS-GÜVENLÝK STOÐU ALTINDA KALANLAR ve KALMAYANLAR
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi



Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=ESONSER_KOD	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ESONSER_KOD=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"HM","")	
FIELDNAME1=ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim sel		
Set sel=Doc.Select1("_STOK46T","STOK46T","KOD='"+trim(RAPOR.KOD)+"'&ACIKKAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
if sel.getreccount=0 thEN
	RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=0
else
	sel.groupby("KOD")
	RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=sel.SF_MIKTAR(1)-sel.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(1)
	if RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK<0 then
		RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=0 	
	end if
end if
Set sel=Nothing	ACIKLAMA=Açýk Satýnalma Sip.	
FIELDNAME1=EKSIK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.EKSIK = RAPOR.ESONSER_KOD - RAPOR.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU	
FIELDNAME1=MPS	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim MPS10,i

Set MPS10=Doc.Select1("dsafsdfg","MMPS10TM","MAMULSTOKKODU='"&RAPOR.KOD&"'&ACIK_KAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
IF MPS10.getreccount<>0 then
	For i= 1 To MPS10.GetrecCount
		RAPOR.MPS=RAPOR.MPS+MPS10.SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR(i)
	Next
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=TALEP	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim ST
Set ST=Doc.Select1("_STOK47T","STOK47T","KOD='"+trim(RAPOR.KOD)+"'&ACIKKAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
if st.getreccount=0 tHEN
	RAPOR.TALEP=0
else
	ST.groupby("KOD")
	RAPOR.TALEP=ST.SF_MIKTAR
	if RAPOR.TALEP<0 then
		RAPOR.TALEP=0 	
	end if
end if
Set ST=Nothing

FIELDNAME1=REZERV	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim S01
Set S01=Doc.Select1("__sdf__","STOK01","KOD='"&RAPOR.KOD&"'&AMBCODE<>'HURDA'&ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='I2'","",-1)
RAPOR.REZERV=S01.SF_MIKTAR

	RAPOR.TIPI="Üretilen"

	RAPOR.TIPI="Satýnalýnan"

	RAPOR.TIPI="Fason"
END IF

----------------------------------------------------------------
Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : STOK-03Y
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK00
Aciklama : YGS-GÜVENLYK STO?U ALTINDA KALANLAR ve KALMAYANLAR _ SQL _ 18-09-2025
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi

SELECT
	STOK00.KOD,
	CASE STOK00.KART_TIPI
	WHEN 'U' THEN N'Üretilen'
	WHEN 'S' THEN N'Satýnalýnan'
	WHEN 'F' THEN N'Fason'
	END AS TIPI,
	STOK00.GK_9,
	STOK00.GK_5,
	STOK00.GK_15,
	STOK00.AD,
	STOK00.NAME2,
	STOK00.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU,
	STOK00.B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU,
	S01.ESONSER_KOD,

	(ISNULL(S01.ESONSER_KOD, 0) - STOK00.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU) AS EKSIK,
	ISNULL(S46T.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK, 0) AS ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK,
	ISNULL(S47T.TALEP, 0)                   AS TALEP,
	ISNULL(M10TM.MPS, 0)                    AS MPS,
	ISNULL(S01.GKD, 0)                      AS GKD,
	ISNULL(S01.IADE, 0)                     AS IADE,
	ISNULL(S01.SILME, 0)                    AS SILME,
	ISNULL(S01.TLS, 0)                      AS TLS,
	ISNULL(S01.KESMEBUKME, 0)               AS KESMEBUKME,
	ISNULL(S01.FASON, 0)                    AS FASON,
	ISNULL(S01.FSONAR, 0)                   AS FSONAR,
	ISNULL(S01.BTIPI, 0)                    AS BTIPI,
	ISNULL(S01.AGS, 0)                      AS AGS,
	ISNULL(S01.PAS, 0)                      AS PAS,
	ISNULL(S01.SPHZ, 0)                     AS SPHZ,
	ISNULL(S01.ONAR, 0)                     AS ONAR,
	ISNULL(S01.REZERV, 0)                   AS REZERV,

	STOK00.IUNIT

	FROM STOK00


	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	KOD,

	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'HM'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS ESONSER_KOD,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'GKD'  THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS GKD,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'ÝÜD'    AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS IADE,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'SÝLME'  AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS SILME,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'TALAÞL' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS TLS,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'BÜKME'  AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS KESMEBUKME,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'FASON'  AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS FASON,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'FSONAR' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS FSONAR,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'BELKAS' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS BTIPI,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'AGS'    AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS AGS,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'PASKHZ' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS PAS,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'SPGHZR' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)AS SPHZ,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'ONARIM' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS ONAR,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'HM'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE, N'') = N'I2' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                            AS REZERV
	FROM STOK01
	GROUP BY KOD
	) AS S01 ON S01.KOD = STOK00.KOD

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	KOD,
	SUM(COALESCE(SF_MIKTAR, 0)) - SUM(COALESCE(SF_NETKAPANANMIK, 0)) AS ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK
	FROM STOK46T
	WHERE ISNULL(ACIKKAPALI, N'') <> N'K'
	GROUP BY KOD
	) AS S46T ON S46T.KOD = STOK00.KOD

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	KOD,
	SUM(COALESCE(SF_MIKTAR, 0)) AS TALEP
	FROM STOK47T
	WHERE ISNULL(ACIKKAPALI, N'') <> N'K'
	GROUP BY KOD
	) AS S47T ON S47T.KOD = STOK00.KOD

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	MAMULSTOKKODU,
	SUM(COALESCE(SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR, 0)) AS MPS
	FROM MMPS10TM
	WHERE ISNULL(ACIK_KAPALI, N'') <> N'K'
	GROUP BY MAMULSTOKKODU
	) AS M10TM ON M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU = STOK00.KOD
	
	WHERE 1=1
	AND STOK00.CODETYPE = 'D'
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	AND STOK00.KOD>=###KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
	AND STOK00.KOD<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_5###
	AND STOK00.GK_5=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_5###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_9###
	AND STOK00.GK_9=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_9###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_15###
	AND STOK00.GK_15=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_15###
	###ENDIF###

Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=KOD	KRITERTIPI=><	ARASTIRFORMULU=STOK00	
FIELDNAME1=GK_5	KRITERTIPI==	
FIELDNAME1=GK_9	KRITERTIPI==	
FIELDNAME1=GK_15	KRITERTIPI==

Hangi Tablo icin : STOK00
Aciklama : YGS-GÜVENLYK STO?U ALTINDA KALANLAR ve KALMAYANLAR _ SQL _ 29-09-2025 _ ATANAMAYAN

SELECT 
	KOD,
	TIPI,
	GK_9,
	GK_5,
	GK_15,
	AD,
	NAME2,
	B_GUVENLIKSTOGU,
	B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU,
	ESONSER_KOD,
	IUNIT,
	SUM(ATANAMAYAN) ATANAMAYAN,
	MAX(EKSIK) EKSIK,
	max(ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK) ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK,
	max(TALEP) TALEP,
	max(MPS) MPS,
	max(GKD) GKD,
	max(IADE) IADE,
	max(SILME) SILME,
	max(TLS) TLS,
	max(KESMEBUKME) KESMEBUKME,
	max(FASON) FASON,
	max(FSONAR) FSONAR,
	max(BTIPI) BTIPI,
	max(AGS) AGS,
	max(PAS) PAS,
	max(SPHZ) SPHZ,
	max(ONAR) ONAR,
	max(REZERV) REZERV,
	max(BELMNT) BELMNT,
	MAX(BOYMNT) BOYMNT,
	max(SPG) SPG,
	max(SPGHZR) SPGHZR,
	max(MEKNK) MEKNK,
	max(ELEKTR) ELEKTR,
	max(GLVGÝD) GLVGÝD,
	max(GLVELK) GLVELK,
	max(ROUTER) ROUTER,

	(ESONSER_KOD + max(ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK) + max(TALEP) + max(MPS) + max(GKD) +  
max(IADE) + max(SILME) + max(TLS) + max(KESMEBUKME) + max(FASON) + max(FSONAR) + max(BTIPI) + max(PAS) + max(SPHZ) +
max(ONAR) + max(REZERV) + max(BELMNT) + MAX(BOYMNT) + max(SPG) + max(SPGHZR) +
max(MEKNK) + max(ELEKTR) + max(GLVGÝD) + max(GLVELK) + max(ROUTER)) DEPOTOPLAM 

FROM 

(
SELECT
	STOK00.KOD,
	CASE STOK00.KART_TIPI
		WHEN 'U' THEN N'Üretilen'
		WHEN 'S' THEN N'Satýnalýnan'
		WHEN 'F' THEN N'Fason'
	END AS TIPI,

	STOK00.GK_9,
	STOK00.GK_5,
	STOK00.GK_15,
	STOK00.AD,
	STOK00.NAME2,
	STOK00.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU,
	STOK00.B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU,
	S01.ESONSER_KOD,

	CASE WHEN M10T2.EVRAKNO <> '' THEN
		ATANAMAYANDEGER
	END ATANAMAYAN,

	(ISNULL(S01.ESONSER_KOD, 0) - STOK00.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU) AS EKSIK,
	ISNULL(S46T.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK, 0) AS ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK,
	ISNULL(S47T.TALEP, 0)                   AS TALEP,
	ISNULL(M10TM.MPS, 0)                    AS MPS,
	ISNULL(S01.GKD, 0)                      AS GKD,
	ISNULL(S01.IADE, 0)                     AS IADE,
	ISNULL(S01.SILME, 0)                    AS SILME,
	ISNULL(S01.TLS, 0)                      AS TLS,
	ISNULL(S01.KESMEBUKME, 0)               AS KESMEBUKME,
	ISNULL(S01.FASON, 0)AS FASON,
	ISNULL(S01.FSONAR, 0)                   AS FSONAR,
	ISNULL(S01.BTIPI, 0)                    AS BTIPI,
	ISNULL(S01.AGS, 0)                      AS AGS,
	ISNULL(S01.PAS, 0)                      AS PAS,
	ISNULL(S01.SPHZ, 0)                     AS SPHZ,
	ISNULL(S01.ONAR, 0)                     AS ONAR,
	ISNULL(S01.REZERV, 0)                   AS REZERV,
	ISNULL(S01.BELMNT, 0)                   AS BELMNT,
	ISNULL(S01.BOYMNT, 0)                   AS BOYMNT,
	ISNULL(S01.SPG, 0)                   AS SPG,
	ISNULL(S01.SPGHZR, 0)                   AS SPGHZR,
	ISNULL(S01.MEKNK, 0)                   AS MEKNK,
	ISNULL(S01.ELEKTR, 0)                   AS ELEKTR,
	ISNULL(S01.GLVGÝD, 0)                   AS GLVGÝD,
	ISNULL(S01.GLVELK, 0)                   AS GLVELK,
	ISNULL(S01.ROUTER, 0)                   AS ROUTER,

	STOK00.IUNIT

	FROM STOK00

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	KOD,

	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'BELMNT'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS BELMNT,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'BOYMNT'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS BOYMNT,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'SPG'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS SPG,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'SPGHZR'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS SPGHZR,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'MEKNK'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS MEKNK,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'ELEKTR'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS ELEKTR,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'GLVGÝD'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS GLVGÝD,
	SUM(CASEWHEN AMBCODE = N'GLVELK'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS GLVELK,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'ROUTER'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS ROUTER,

	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'HM'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS ESONSER_KOD,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'GKD'  THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS GKD,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'ÝÜD'    AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS IADE,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'SÝLME'  AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS SILME,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'TALAÞL' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)AS TLS,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'BÜKME'  AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS KESMEBUKME,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'FASON'  AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS FASON,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'FSONAR' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS FSONAR,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'BELKAS' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS BTIPI,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'AGS'    AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS AGS,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'PASKHZ' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS PAS,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'SPGHZR' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS SPHZ,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'ONARIM' AND ISNULL(ALLOC_SYSSTATUS, N'') = N'' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                              AS ONAR,
	SUM(CASE WHEN AMBCODE = N'HM'     AND ISNULL(ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE, N'') = N'I2' THEN SF_MIKTAR END)                                            AS REZERV
	
	FROM STOK01

	GROUP BY KOD

	) AS S01 ON S01.KOD = STOK00.KOD

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	KOD,
	SUM(COALESCE(SF_MIKTAR, 0)) - SUM(COALESCE(SF_NETKAPANANMIK, 0)) AS ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK
	FROM STOK46T
	WHERE ISNULL(ACIKKAPALI, N'') <> N'K'

	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	AND KOD>=###KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
	AND KOD<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
	###ENDIF###

	GROUP BY KOD
	) AS S46T ON S46T.KOD = STOK00.KOD

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	KOD,
	SUM(COALESCE(SF_MIKTAR, 0)) AS TALEP
	FROM STOK47T
	WHERE ISNULL(ACIKKAPALI, N'') <> N'K'
	GROUP BY KOD
	) AS S47T ONS47T.KOD = STOK00.KOD

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
	MAMULSTOKKODU,
	SUM(COALESCE(SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR, 0)) AS MPS
	FROM MMPS10TM
	WHERE ISNULL(ACIK_KAPALI, N'') <> N'K'
	GROUP BY MAMULSTOKKODU
	) AS M10TM ON M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU = STOK00.KOD
	
	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
		EVRAKNO, 
		R_SIRANO, 
		R_KAYNAKKODU,
		(R_MIKTAR0-((R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN+R_HM_ODONEMNET) - R_HM_IADE_EDILEN)) ATANAMAYANDEGER

	FROM MMPS10T

	LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00X ON S00X.KOD =  MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU

	WHERE 1=1
		AND R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'
		AND R_ACIK_KAPALI<>'K'
		AND R_BOM_PLANOPS<>'F'
		AND R_BOM_PLANOPS<>'B'
		AND S00X.GK_6 <> 'KYOK' 

	) AS M10T ON M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU = STOK00.KOD

	LEFT JOIN (
	SELECT
		EVRAKNO,
		R_SIRANO
	FROM MMPS10T
	
	WHERE 1=1
		AND R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'
		AND R_ACIK_KAPALI<>'K'
	) AS M10T2 ON M10T2.EVRAKNO = M10T.EVRAKNO AND  M10T2.R_SIRANO = M10T.R_SIRANO


	WHERE 1=1
	AND STOK00.CODETYPE = 'D'
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	AND STOK00.KOD>=###KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
	AND STOK00.KOD<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_5###
	AND STOK00.GK_5=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_5###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_9###
	AND STOK00.GK_9=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_9###
	###ENDIF###
	
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_15###
	AND STOK00.GK_15=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_GK_15###
	###ENDIF###

)XXX

	GROUP BY
	KOD,
	TIPI,
	GK_9,
	GK_5,
	GK_15,
	AD,
	NAME2,
	B_GUVENLIKSTOGU,
	B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU,
	ESONSER_KOD,
	IUNIT

FIELDNAME1=GK_9	KRITERTIPI==	
FIELDNAME1=GK_15	KRITERTIPI==

Baglantili Tablolar Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=GK_5	JOINEDTABLE=GECOUST	JOINEDFIELD=EVRAKNO	RESULTFIELD=KOD	


Ekran Formu Sekmesi



Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi


FIELDNAME1=ESONSER_KOD	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ESONSER_KOD=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"HM","")	
FIELDNAME1=ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim sel		
Set sel=Doc.Select1("_STOK46T","STOK46T","KOD='"+trim(RAPOR.KOD)+"'&ACIKKAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
if sel.getreccount=0 thEN
	RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=0
else
	sel.groupby("KOD")
	RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=sel.SF_MIKTAR(1)-sel.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(1)
	if RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK<0 then
		RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=0 	
	end if
end if
Set sel=Nothing	ACIKLAMA=Açýk Satýnalma Sip.	
FIELDNAME1=EKSIK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.EKSIK = RAPOR.ESONSER_KOD - RAPOR.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU	
FIELDNAME1=HM	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.HM=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"HM","")	ACIKLAMA=HM DEPO	
FIELDNAME1=MPS	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim MPS10,i

Set MPS10=Doc.Select1("dsafsdfg","MMPS10TM","MAMULSTOKKODU='"&RAPOR.KOD&"'&ACIK_KAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
IF MPS10.getreccount<>0 then
	For i= 1 To MPS10.GetrecCount
		RAPOR.MPS=RAPOR.MPS+MPS10.SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR(i)
	Next
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=TALEP	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim ST
Set ST=Doc.Select1("_STOK47T","STOK47T","KOD='"+trim(RAPOR.KOD)+"'&ACIKKAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
if st.getreccount=0 tHEN
	RAPOR.TALEP=0
else
	ST.groupby("KOD")
	RAPOR.TALEP=ST.SF_MIKTAR
	if RAPOR.TALEP<0 then
		RAPOR.TALEP=0 	
	end if
end if
Set ST=Nothing	
FIELDNAME1=GKD	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.GKD=Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(RAPOR.KOD,"GKD")	
FIELDNAME1=IADE	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.IADE=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"ÝÜD","")	ACIKLAMA=Ýade Depo Mevcudu	
FIELDNAME1=SILME	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.SILME=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"SÝLME","")	
FIELDNAME1=TLS	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TLS=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"TALAÞL","")	ACIKLAMA=Talaþlý Depo Mevcudu	
FIELDNAME1=ONAR	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ONAR=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"ONARIM","")	
FIELDNAME1=REZERV	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim S01
Set S01=Doc.Select1("__sdf__","STOK01","KOD='"&RAPOR.KOD&"'&AMBCODE='HM'&ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='I2'","",-1)
RAPOR.REZERV=S01.SF_MIKTAR	
FIELDNAME1=TIPI	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=IF RAPOR.KART_TIPI="U" THEN
	RAPOR.TIPI="Üretilen"
ELSEIF RAPOR.KART_TIPI="S" THEN
	RAPOR.TIPI="Satýnalýnan"
ELSEIF RAPOR.KART_TIPI="F" THEN
	RAPOR.TIPI="Fason"
END IF	
FIELDNAME1=BXL	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.BXL=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"BXL-MT","")	ACIKLAMA=BXL MOTOR DEPOSU	
FIELDNAME1=TKSR1	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TKSR1=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"TKSR1","")	ACIKLAMA=TEKNIK SERVIS DEPOSU	
FIELDNAME1=TOPLAM	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.TOPLAM=RAPOR.MPS+RAPOR.TALEP+RAPOR.GKD+RAPOR.IADE+RAPOR.BXL+RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK+RAPOR.REZERV+RAPOR.HM	ACIKLAMA=TOPLAM	
FIELDNAME1=FARK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.FARK=RAPOR.TOPLAM-RAPOR.B_GUVENLIKSTOGU	ACIKLAMA=FARK

Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi


FIELDNAME1=ESONSER_KOD	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=RAPOR.ESONSER_KOD=Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(RAPOR.KOD,"HM","")	
FIELDNAME1=ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=Dim sel		
Set sel=Doc.Select1("_STOK46T","STOK46T","KOD='"+trim(RAPOR.KOD)+"'&ACIKKAPALI<>'K'","",-1)
if sel.getreccount=0 thEN
	RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=0
else
	sel.groupby("KOD")
	RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=sel.SF_MIKTAR(1)-sel.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(1)
	if RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK<0 then
		RAPOR.ACIK_SATINALMA_SIP_MIK=0 	
	end if
end if
Set sel=Nothing	ACIKLAMA=Açýk Satýnalma Sip.

		RAPOR.MPS=RAPOR.MPS+MPS10.SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR(i)
	Next
END IF

----------------------------------------------------------------
Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : STOK29T-01
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK29T
Aciklama : RED TANIMLI SATINALMA ÝRSALÝYELERÝ
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi

SELECT DISTINCT
  s.EVRAKNO,
  s.KOD,
  s.TL_FIYAT,
  t.QCONAY,
  t.TL_FIYAT
FROM STOK29T s
JOIN STOK29T t
  ON s.EVRAKNO = t.EVRAKNO
 AND s.KOD     = t.KOD
WHERE s.QCONAY = 'I'
  AND (t.QCONAY <> 'I' OR t.QCONAY IS NULL)
  AND ISNULL(s.TL_FIYAT, -99999999) <> ISNULL(t.TL_FIYAT, -99999999)
ORDER BY s.EVRAKNO, s.KOD;

Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi



Baglantili Tablolar Sekmesi



Ekran Formu Sekmesi



Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi

Eklenen Alanlar - Hesaplamalar Sekmesi


FIELDNAME1=DV	HESAPLAMA_SCRIPT=IF RAPOR.TARIH < KRITERGIRIS.BTARIH OR RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE = "0S" THEN
	RAPOR.DV = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
END IF
IF RAPOR.TARIH >= KRITERGIRIS.BTARIH THEN
	SELECT CASE TRIM(RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE)
		CASE "SI"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)<>"1" THEN
				RAPOR.G1 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.GIRIS1=1
			END IF
		CASE "RI"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)="1" THEN
				RAPOR.C1=RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.CIKIS1=1				
			END IF
		CASE "SF"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)<>"1" THEN
				RAPOR.G2 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.GIRIS2=1
			END IF
		CASE "IF"
			IF TRIM(RAPOR.IADEMI)= "1" THEN
				RAPOR.C2=RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
				RAPOR.CIKIS2=1				
			END IF
		CASE "I2"
			RAPOR.G3 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.GIRIS3=1
		CASE "TR"
			RAPOR.G4 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.GIRIS4=1
		CASE "FG"
			RAPOR.G5 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.GIRIS5=1
		CASE "UT"
			RAPOR.C3 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS3=1				
		CASE "F2"
			RAPOR.C4 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS4=1				
		CASE "YC"
			RAPOR.C5 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS5=1				
		CASE "II"
			RAPOR.C6 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS6=1				
		CASE "T2"
			RAPOR.C7 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
			RAPOR.CIKIS7=1				
		CASE "DG"
			RAPOR.C8 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
		CASE "DT"
			RAPOR.C9 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
		CASE "KD"
			RAPOR.C10 = RAPOR.SF_MIKTAR
	END SELECT	
END IF	ACIKLAMA=Devir Mik	
FIELDNAME1=G1	
FIELDNAME1=G2

SELECT MUHDST.EVRAKTYPE, MUHDST.EVRAKNO, MUHDST.TRNUM, MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE, MUHDST.MUHASEBEFIYAT,OR_TRNUM
FROM MUHDST
WHERE 1=1 
AND (1=1 
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL01_EVRAKTYPE###
	OR MUHDST.EVRAKTYPE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL01_EVRAKTYPE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL02_EVRAKTYPE###
	OR MUHDST.EVRAKTYPE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL02_EVRAKTYPE###
	###ENDIF###
	)
AND (1=1 
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL01_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL01_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL02_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL02_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL03_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL03_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL04_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL04_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL05_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL05_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL06_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL06_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL07_MUHASEBECODE###
	OR MUHDST.MUHASEBECODE=###KRITERGIRIS.CL07_MUHASEBECODE###
	###ENDIF###
	)

	Oran = 8

	Oran = 18

	RAPOR.MATRAH=RAPOR.MUHASEBEFIYAT

	RAPOR.TARIH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI20E", "TARIH", RAPOR.EVRAKNO, "")
	RAPOR.ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI20T", "ACIKLAMA", RAPOR.EVRAKNO+TRIM(RAPOR.OR_TRNUM),"EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	RAPOR.CH_KODU = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI20T", "KOD", RAPOR.EVRAKNO+TRIM(RAPOR.OR_TRNUM),"EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
 	RAPOR.CH_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", RAPOR.CH_KODU, "")
 	RAPOR.VD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", RAPOR.CH_KODU, "")
 	RAPOR.VNO = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", RAPOR.CH_KODU, "")
Elseif RAPOR.EVRAKTYPE = "NK" Then
	RAPOR.EVRTARIH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("NAKT11E", "TARIH", RAPOR.EVRAKNO, "")
	RAPOR.TARIH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("NAKT11E", "TARIH", RAPOR.EVRAKNO, "")
	RAPOR.ACIKLAMA = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("NAKT10T", "ACIKLAMA", RAPOR.EVRAKNO+TRIM(RAPOR.OR_TRNUM),"EVRAKNO+TRNUM")

Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : Z000000002
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK10A
Aciklama : ÜRÜN AÐICINDA KULLANILAN MALÝYETLÝ MALÝYETSÝZ HAMMADDELER
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi

SELECT 
    S.AMBCODE,
    S.KOD,
	S.AD,
    F.FOKOD,
    S.URMIK,
    F.TIP,F.DIPKOD,F.DIPAD,F.BIRIM,F.KULMIK
	
FROM
    (
SELECT AMBCODE, H.KOD , K.AD,H.FOKOD,   SUM(SF_MIKTAR) AS URMIK
     FROM STOK10A H INNER JOIN  STOK00 K ON H.KOD=K.KOD

	 WHERE 1=1 
        AND H.AMBCODE>=###KRITERGIRIS.B_AMBCODE###
	###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_AMBCODE###
	AND H.AMBCODE<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_AMBCODE###
	###ENDIF###
    
          ###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	  AND H.KOD>=###KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
	  ###ENDIF###
	
	  ###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
	  AND H.KOD<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
        ###ENDIF###
       
          ###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_TARIH###
	     AND TARIH<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_TARIH###
	    ###ENDIF###


       
        ###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_FOKOD###
	  AND H.FOKOD=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_FOKOD###
	  ###ENDIF###





    GROUP BY H.KOD, FOKOD, AMBCODE,H.KOD,K.AD
   HAVING SUM(SF_MIKTAR) > 0
) AS S

CROSS APPLY fn_Agac_V1(S.AMBCODE,S.KOD,S.AD, S.FOKOD,S.URMIK) F

Kriter Tanimlari Sekmesi

FIELDNAME1=TARIH	KRITERTIPI=<=	SORUACIKLAMA=Envanter Tarihi (Son Durum Ýçin Boþ Býrakýnýz)

FIELDNAME1=FOKOD	KRITERTIPI==	ARASTIRFORMULU=FOSYSFT	
FIELDNAME1=AMBCODE	KRITERTIPI=><	ARASTIRFORMULU=GDEF00	
FIELDNAME1=STTARSEC	KRITERTIPI=?	SORUACIKLAMA=Standart Maliyet Geçerlilik Tarihi	DEFVALUE01=Doc.Bugun()	
FIELDNAME1=STMALSEC	KRITERTIPI=?	SORUACIKLAMA=Hammadde Maliyetlendirme Seçeneði (Endeks)	DEFVALUE01=*L	
FIELDNAME1=STOPESEC	KRITERTIPI=?	SORUACIKLAMA=Operasyon Maliyetlendirme Seçeneði (Endeks)	DEFVALUE01=FE	
FIELDNAME1=PABIR	KRITERTIPI=?	DEFVALUE01=TL

Rapor Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OZAK_2026 / Uygulama : REP17S
Makro Kodu : Z000000003
Hangi Tablo icin : STOK10A
Aciklama : Envanter Analizi (SQL)
Prosedur Sekmesi : 



SQL Komutu Sekmesi

SELECT STOK10A.KOD, STOK10A.LOTNUMBER, STOK10A.SERINO, STOK10A.AMBCODE, STOK10A.LOCATION1, STOK10A.LOCATION2, 
STOK10A.LOCATION3, STOK10A.LOCATION4, SUM(STOK10A.SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, STOK10A.SF_UNIT, STOK10A.VRI_VR_C, 
STOK10A.VRI_VR_R1, STOK10A.VRI_VR_R2, STOK10A.VRI_VR_R3, STOK10A.VRI_NUM1, STOK10A.VRI_NUM2, STOK10A.FOKOD,
SUM(STOK10A.TL_TUTAR) TL_TUTAR, SUM(STOK10A.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR) TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR, STOK00.AD AD, STOK00.GK_1 GK_1,
STOK00.GK_2 GK_2, STOK00.GK_3 GK_3,STOK00.GK_4 GK_4, STOK00.GK_5 GK_5,STOK00.GK_6 GK_6,STOK00.GK_7 GK_7,STOK00.GK_8 GK_8,
STOK00.GK_9 GK_9,STOK00.GK_10 GK_10, SUM(STOK10A.TL_TUTAR)+SUM(STOK10A.TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR) MALIYET
FROM STOK10A
INNER JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK10A.KOD

WHERE 1=1

 AND STOK10A.KOD>=###KRITERGIRIS.B_KOD###
###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
And STOK10A.KOD<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_KOD###
###ENDIF###
